The History of Italy, Vol. 3 (Francesco Guicciardini (Austin Parke Goddard, trans.), 1561)
==========================================================
Guicciardini's Storia d'Italia (written 1537-40, first printed 1561) - the first 'modern' analytical history; the Italian wars as the tragedy of a divided peninsula. Goddard's English translation (1753-61, 10 vols.). Vol. 3 of 10.

Chevalier AusTIN PARKE GoDDARD,
Knight of the Military Order of St. Stephen.
